function J4(e, t = Date.now()) {
  if (e === null) {
    return "permanent";
  }
  const n = (typeof e == "number" ? e : e.getTime()) - t;
  if (n <= 0) {
    return "expired";
  }
  const i = Math.floor(n / vS);
  const r = Math.floor(n % vS / Dx);

}
